Its protest over the destruction of the [[Katabasion]] remained pending, and the commission found that the College had received reports of the Archflamen's departures from the liturgy throughout his tour of the theatre and had welcomed them, since attendance at the vat-rites had risen wherever he preached. It found that the College had made no inquiry into the Archflamen's household, his advisors or the eleven thousand who followed his crawlers, and that it had confirmed [[Ilan Dace|Haruspex Ilan Dace]] in his office on the Archflamen's recommendation without examination. The College's delegation to the Conclave was dissolved. The words "He knows me", which the Archflamen's followers had used to answer the vat-rite, were proscribed in every hold of the theatre, and the [[Kindlers]]' creed, recovered in fragments from the Tabularium's records, was sealed by the Hunt. The office of Archflamen of the Tartarean theatre was left vacant and has not been filled.

The Répertoire, for its part, revised its method. From the close of Campaign Record 40-PA, every order of the Tartarus front that set a ceiling on null transfers carried with it an obligation to refer any breach of the ceiling to the Command for decision within the rotation, and the null list posted in the recovery halls at Watchpoint Hades was made a matter of the Command's own record. The vat-crews' new category for soldiers returned after being logged null was retained, though no further returns were entered under it after the siege. Relay chains at forty-metre intervals, on the pattern Verrill Chase had laid under the cone, were adopted for every gallery and deep sap of the Tartarus front by order of the Hephaestean sapper companies in the following rotation. The order credits the pattern to Verrill Chase, relay engineer attached to the Thirty-Fifth Trench Regiment.

The Third Siege Army itself did not survive the peace in its old form. Eleven of its Trench Regiments were disbanded, and their soldiers, many of them returning from the vats after rotations held on the Mast, were posted to the holds of the upper depths. The Thirty-Third Trench Regiment, sent to the Grid Delta Wedge in Rotation Four, remained there and was transferred formally to its command. The memorial to the Quiet Ones on the ceiling at [[Landing Shelf Kappa]] was extended to carry the names of the labour corps dead of the whole siege, sixty thousand in all.

Depth 40 was interdicted. No hold stands on the Cinder Flats, and no Imperial vessel holds station in the ceiling above them.

Two CODE 18 queries from Rotation Eighteen remain open, both concerning an authority logged as UNREGISTERED, CLASS UNKNOWN, CARRYING NO SEAL. The Hunt of the Director has requested that they remain open.

Verdict: Closed. Ave Imperator.

---

## Statistics

Figures are those of the [[Répertoire du Savoir Athénien|Répertoire]] as given in the sections above. A transfer is one death and re-embodiment of an implanted soldier; a null is a transfer that never reached the vats at [[Watchpoint Hades]].

### Campaign totals

| Measure | Figure |
|---|---|
| Duration | Eighteen rotations by the theatre's clock; ten rotations and five months of front time |
| Transfers, Third Siege Army | 88,600,000 |
| Nulls logged | 4,310,000 (ceiling set at 3 percent), all held by the [[Echo Mast]] and released |
| Defenders | About 6,200,000 souls on the spur, all dead or taken |
| Arsenal output before the revolt | 81 percent of the theatre's keyed divine mortar and aedificant shells |
| Ceiling-lances | 60 in the spur and inner belt, 10 in the second belt |
| Cost of one transfer | 400 litres of nectar, 11 seconds of Watchpoint bandwidth, 9 days of vat time |
| [[Quiet Ones]] labour corps | 420,000 bondsmen; 4,000 km of track laid; 60,000 dead |
| Cargo lowered to Kappa, first five months | About 11,000,000 tonnes |

### Losses by phase

| Phase | Section | Transfers | Nulls | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| First assault on the outer belt (Rot. 1, Cyc. 79 to 85) | 2 | 184,000 | 206 | Fourteenth Cohort: 571 of 640 lost in the first watch |
| Reduction of the outer belt (to Rot. 2, Cyc. 381) | 2 | 960,000 | 4,140 | Estimate 900,000; 18 months against 8 |
| Trench-line attrition, Rotation One | 2 | about 2,300 a cycle | | No regiment attacking |
| Crisis of the Fifth (Rot. 3, Cyc. 211 onward) | 3 | 76,000 transferred or captured | 4,200 | Forty-First Trench Regiment destroyed; Arsenal losses about 41,000 and 91 tanks |
| The Saturation (Rot. 4, Cyc. 5 to 9) | 3 | 312,000 | 11,000 | 300 metres gained on two leaves |
| Raid on Ceiling-Lance Seven (Rot. 5, Cyc. 402) | 3 | 640 | 31 | 19 of 260 breachers returned |
| Running total, end of Rotation Five | 3 | a little over 5,000,000 | about 190,000 | |
| The Big Push (sixty cycles) | 5 | 2,900,000 | 180,000 | Planned 1,800,000; about 320,000 Arsenal dead |
| Landings, first cycle (Rotation Eight) | 6 | 204,000 | 19,000 | Highest single-cycle loss to that date |
| Battle of the Demus Rim | 6 | 31,000 | 4,000 | 74 tanks lost, 12 of them Colossi |
| Kastellan Offensive, northern inner belt (five cycles) | 7 | 670,000 | | 400 to 900 metres gained per leaf |
| First battle for Vault 9 | 8 | about 9,000, plus more than 2,000 on the Deep List | | |
| First assault on the breach | 9 | 11,000 | 900 | Thirty-Fifth lost 19 of 30 Hoplons |
| Assaults on the breach, Ninth Assault Phalanx | 9 | about 600,000 | | Five assaults |
| Leaf 57-44 after the Great Mine, all fighting | 9 | more than 1,200,000 | 61,000 | Operation Scaean Gate: 90 of the [[Achillean Order]] in, 31 out |
| The hook, Fifth Line Phalanx (eight cycles) | 10 | 240,000 | 61,000 | 9,000 logged absent |
| Assault on the Scree, first eight cycles | 11 | 160,000 | 39,000 | Nearly one in four null; Twenty-Second lost 58,000 |
| Assault on the Scree, full ten cycles | 12 | 221,000 | | Minerva and Verrill arrive Cycle 88 |
| Founders' Gate diversion | 12 | 31,000 | | One Behemoth and 11 of the Order's tanks lost |
| Three gates and the outer ward (nineteen cycles) | 12 | 104,000 | fewer than 900 | Estimate 400,000 |
